[       script:ox_lib] State bags are used to replicate entity/player data between server and clients.
[       script:ox_lib] When strict mode is OFF, clients are able to freely modify state bag values.
[       script:ox_lib] 
[       script:ox_lib] Enabling strict mode improves security by rejecting replicated state bag modifications from clients.
